On the first level, we introduce the following: that the State guarantees the level of old age coverage is not lower than the subsistence minimum established by law and protects against poverty. The level of pension of a solidarity system is determined only by the insurance traineeship and the salary of a citizen. At the second level in the State Pension Insurance System, we're giving you the opportunity to reinvest in public infrastructure projects. At the same time, the State guarantees that insured persons will be reimbursed in the form of pensions, taking into account a citizen’s pension scheme. And on the third level, we're introducing, so-called, pension programs for companies that have none.
